Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The riemannian variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 177 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 943 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 177 + O(1/n^943). Summing over all modes and applying the riemannian convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
